```text
+
+### #199の送信境界・冪等性：統合側の隔離参照契約
+
+根拠は [Muse監査の追加受入条件](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7056344891) と [#199の未報告依頼](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/199#issuecomment-7075745505)。外部送信のない合成値だけで、送信境界の意味論を持つ参照モデルを隔離実行した。owner・character・session・turn、材料manifest、許可版を合成値で束縛し、最終送信境界で許可版と冪等キーを照合する。
+
+| ケース | 期待 | 結果 |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| 許可版が変わらない対照 | 1回送信 | 1回 |
+| 最終再照合後・送信境界前に共有を撤回 | 古い許可版を拒否、0回 | `stale-permission`、0回 |
+| 同一turnの送信再試行 | 重複を拒否し総数1回 | 1回目 `sent`、2回目 `duplicate-turn`、総数1回 |
+| 送信境界が撤回より先に確定する順序 | 線形化順序に従い1回 | 1回 |
+| 撤回が送信境界より先に確定する順序 | 線形化順序に従い0回 | 0回 |
+
+5ケース成功、失敗0。これは統合側の参照契約モデルであり、#199の実コード・DBトランザクション・キュー・ネットワーク送信は実行していない。実経路のTOCTOU原子性、正本の許可版照合、同一turnの永続冪等性は引き続き未検証で、#199担当の報告待ち。今回の合成試験を実経路の受入・本番承認に読み替えない。
+
+#194は同HEADのActionsがないためCI未確認のまま。実Gemini、実価格・モデル仕様、認証、DB、会話API接続、Body Clock同時実行、感情の定期回収、iPhone Safariの本番受入は、設計・合成試験・CI・main統合とは別の未完了段階として維持する。

+### 未決のまま維持する判断
+
+Issue #222の実態監査にある関係性3系統（ポイント制・5軸v1.1・感情／行動v2）は、現行コード上で単一の正本・矛盾時の優先順位が宣言されていない。どれかを正本に選ぶ、合成する、または用途別に併存させる判断は未合意のまま。#219の材料契約で同一の「関係性」材料として扱う前に、所有者の仕様判断が必要である。v1.1の本番有効状態、Body Clockとチャットの生活文脈、正式characterId、DBドリフト、`consume_daily_message` の定義、#194のCI、実Gemini・認証・本番・実機受入も未確認または未決。
+
+今回、新しい契約差分はなく、既存文書に記録済みの隔離合成試験を再利用した。新しいfixtureの追加・担当ブランチ変更・実API／DB／本番接続は行っていない。設計、隔離試験、CI、main統合、本番、実機を別段階として維持する。

+## 2026-11-24 追補：関係性の正本決定・構造改善の方向・進行順
+
+この追補は、上記の2026-11-21時点の「関係性3系統の正本・優先順位は未決」という記録を、以下のユーザー決定について更新するものです。過去の監査結果と判断履歴は削除しません。
+
+### 1. 関係性3系統の正本（ユーザー決定）
+
+- **5軸エンジン v1.1 を関係性の正本とする。** 5軸の状態と関係ステータス（例：`romantic_partner`）だけが、関係性の状態を宣言する。
+- **ポイント系は従属層とする。** 5軸へ渡す候補入力と、画面表示用の進み具合メーターに限る。単独でステージ・関係ステータスを宣言しない。
+- **感情・行動 v2 は表現層として独立させる。** そのターンの感情・態度を表し、5軸の正本状態を距離感の土台として読む。関係ステータスを書き換えない。
+- **結婚は `none → romantic_partner → marriage` の先に置く。** 告白と同じ critical 検証経路を使う方針までを決定済み。結婚の成立条件・排他台帳・原子的確定の詳細は未設計であり、実装指示ではない。
+- 2026-11-24の読み取り専用DB監査では、2026-10-02〜10-09に v1.1 の稼働、3日パターン完成から軸適用まで、告白から `lover`／`romantic_partner` への遷移実績が確認された。ポイント値は同期間0だった。一方、**現在の環境値・現在の稼働は未確認**。この監査はMuseの報告であり、統合担当による本番接続・DB確認ではない。
+- 根拠：[本番実績の読み取り監査](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7118640197) · [正本のユーザー決定](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7118688330)
+
+### 2. 隔離合成契約の範囲と限界
+
+`tests/relationship-source-of-truth-contract.test.cjs` は、合成 owner／character IDだけを使う契約投影テストです。5軸のみが軸・ステータスを供給すること、ポイントは候補入力／表示メーターに留まること、感情・行動はターン表現に留まること、正本欠落・owner／character不一致をfail-closedにすること、高いromance値だけでは恋人・結婚を成立させないことを確認します。アプリの実経路、DB、認証、外部サービスは呼び出しません。
+
+対象HEAD `6755c477105bfcd431abb0f9bcefeeffebb5b2cd` の `Canonical relationship integration tests` は **435/435 pass、0 fail**（[Actions](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/actions/runs/45303127348)）。これは合成契約と既存回帰の成功であり、本番の現在値・実経路・DB原子性の証明ではありません。
+
+### 3. ユーザー承認済みの構造改善方向（実装指示ではない）
+
+Museの優先順提案8件は、方向性として全件採用済みです。個別の実装・マージ・本番接続・DB変更は別途承認を要します。
+
+1. 状態層に `characterId` 次元を導入する（まず美咲1人を完成させ、将来の複数キャラで状態が混ざらない基盤）。
+2. チャットとBody Clockが別々に決めているキャラの「今日」を単一の正本へ寄せる。
+3. 結婚の排他を守る世界側台帳と、先着を原子的に確定する仕組みを設計する。
+4. ペルソナfallbackの発生をログ等で可視化し、セッション開始時点の版を固定する。
+5. 約3,000行のチャットルートへの追加を避け、まず#218の材料選択レイヤー分離を優先する。
+6. `consume_daily_message` RPCの定義をリポジトリ管理下へ置き、本番DBとのドリフトを確認する。
+7. 呼び出し元のない死にコードを削除するか、未使用と明示する。
+8. 5軸エンジンの有効版・開始時刻・最終処理時刻・パターン完成件数を読める稼働ステータスの読み口を設ける。
+
+根拠：[8件の改善提案とユーザー承認](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7118781603)
+
+### 4. 進行ルール：実経路の一周を先にする
+
+まず全体を仮組みし、実経路を端から端まで1回通すことを優先します。まだ実経路に接続していない部品の運用・リカバリー・例外意味論を、接続前にさらに深掘りしません。既存の未決論点は削除せず、接続直前フェーズへ送ります。すでに開始済みの隔離検証は破棄しませんが、新しい深掘りは開始しません。交通整理（順番・優先度・深掘り可否）はMuse側の提案をユーザーが決め、[MUSE-AUDIT]として本PRに記録します。
+
+根拠：[一周優先・深掘り停止のユーザー決定](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7118904049)
+
+### 5. 送信境界・発行証明などの未決論点
+
+既存5ケースの参照契約が示すのは、順序を外から与えた場合の判定規則までです。原子性、キャッシュなしの最新許可状態の可視性、manifestを含む全束縛値変更時の無効化、線形化点、永続冪等性、並列確保、プロセス再起動・クラッシュ後のunknown outcome、キー保持期間は未証明です。線形化点を「許可版検証と冪等キー確保を行うトランザクションのcommit」とする案や、確保後の撤回を取り消さない意味論はMuseの監査提案であり、製品仕様としての採否は未決です。
+
+また、メモリ内 `WeakSet` の同一性による発行証明はプロセス境界・直列化を越えられず、実経路ではサーバー側で検証できる発行方式が必要です。#194の合成単価・思考トークン予約試験は統合側の期待契約であり、#194のコードが費用ガードを実装した証拠ではありません。#194の対象HEADのCIも未確認です。
+
+これらは接続直前まで記録に留め、現段階では追加の深掘り・実ストア試験・実Gemini送信を行いません。接続直前に改めて担当実装、最新HEADのCI、実ストアでの競合・再起動証拠を確認します。
+
+根拠：[送信境界の監査](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7118200884) · [統合設計の証拠範囲監査](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7118229136)
+
+### 6. Museとの報告経路
+
+- 各担当の通常報告は、従来どおり統合担当がDraft PR #219へ集約する。
+- **Muse宛ての報告・依頼はIssue #223にも投稿する。** 今後のMuse監査依頼を#219へ直接投稿して自動確認を待つ運用にはしない。
+- 進行順・優先度・深掘り可否の交通整理は、Muse側から#219へ `[MUSE-AUDIT]` として提示する。各担当はその決定に従い、Muse向けの結果を#223へ返す。
+- #223本文は作成時の「Museからの報告は#219」の旧文面が残っているが、最新のユーザー決定である[コメント](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7118960700)を優先する。#223の既存preflightはREVIEW_REQUIREDのため、次の新規依頼・報告を投稿する前に本文・判断記録の整合を確認する。
+
+### 7. 現在の統合境界
+
+この追補で確定したのは設計上の正本・進行方針・構造改善の方向です。担当PRの実装完了、#194のCI、実Gemini、実認証、Body Clock同時実行、感情の定期回収、iPhone Safari、本番DBの現在値、main統合、本番受入はそれぞれ別判定のままです。#199の未報告依頼も完了扱いしません。
+
+## 3ブロック単独一周の隔離検証（Issue #218）
+
+選択肢Aを採用し、`tests/three-block` に純粋モジュールを固定版で取り込み、薄い接続を追加した。通常の `npm test` は `tests/three-block-one-pass.test.ts` の5ケースを実行する。入力・版・モデル応答・送信先はすべて合成で、モデルと送信はメモリ内で記録する。[方向指示](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7152977579) · [準備パッケージ](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/pull/219#issuecomment-7156296685)
+
+| 再利用元（固定HEAD） | 対象HEADの既存CI | ローカル隔離ベースライン |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| #199 `071270a15716a45bf558363d8ef35f1bef30c787` | [成功](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/actions/runs/44640044171) | 材料選別 21/21 |
+| #221 `74780ba0100d629ec7046a097deaecb430b35a30` | [成功](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/actions/runs/44644915107) | プレビュー4件・認可の外部接続なし3件、計7/7。接続にはプレビューだけを使用 |
+| #194 `8d7b732fb19ef45c8148c135fffb94451e9bd9ea` | チェックランの記録なし | オフライン 33/33、合成品質チェック 33/33（7シナリオ） |
+
+統合の変更前HEADは `0bc925c58abf1c678b7deaeac0509d95c33f55ab`。[変更前HEADの既存CI](https://github.com/miyazaki1016/misaki/actions/runs/45305457064)
+
+| 合成ケース | ローカル隔離結果 |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. 通常 | 選別結果、調律後、文字列化を経たモデルスタブ入力、送信記録で契約を照合。候補1件、モデル1件、送信1件 |
+| 2. 行動失敗 | 失敗を伝える平叙文を1件送信。完了を語る対照は `false-completion` で送信0件 |
+| 3. 沈黙 | 空の採用材料と明示的沈黙でモデル0件・送信0件 |
+| 4. 通常復元×沈黙 | 調律後にC→Aを割り込ませても当該ターンはモデル0件・送信0件。既存セッションはC、新規セッションはAを参照 |
+| 5. 質問不可 | 同じ疑問符入り応答で、質問不可は `question-forbidden` で送信0件、質問可の対照は1件 |
+
+調律は既存プレビューで検証された版と設定を使い、選別の承認済み材料・ID付き制約を構造化したまま渡す。#194の本文生成器で履歴の重複記載を省き、送信ガードは文字列化後の合成入力を参照する。通常復元は試験用の承認版ポインタで再現し、発言検査は合成の予約文言・疑問符に限定した。正式な質問判定・発行証明・版互換・TTL・永続化と復元、実経路、#194の費用ガードと実Gemini評価は別工程。

```

### tests/relationship-source-of-truth-contract.test.cjs

```text
diff --git a/tests/relationship-source-of-truth-contract.test.cjs b/tests/relationship-source-of-truth-contract.test.cjs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..139b3865
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/relationship-source-of-truth-contract.test.cjs
@@ -0,0 +1,137 @@
+'use strict';
+
+const { test } = require('node:test');
+const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
+
+const AXES = ['friendship', 'trust', 'playfulness', 'affection', 'romance'];
+
+/**
+ * Contract-only projection for synthetic fixtures.
+ * This is not imported by the application and does not claim to test the
+ * current production route, DB, authentication, or any external service.
+ */
+function projectRelationshipMaterial({ ownerId, characterId, fiveAxis, points, emotionAction }) {
+  const scoped = Boolean(
+    ownerId && characterId && fiveAxis &&
+    fiveAxis.ownerId === ownerId && fiveAxis.characterId === characterId
+  );
+
+  const relationship = scoped
+    ? {
+        characterId,
+        axes: Object.fromEntries(AXES.map((axis) => [axis, fiveAxis.axes[axis]])),
+        status: fiveAxis.status,
+      }
+    : null;
+
+  return {
+    relationship,
+    pointCandidates: Array.isArray(points?.candidateInput) ? [...points.candidateInput] : [],
+    progressMeter: Number.isFinite(points?.progressMeter) ? points.progressMeter : null,
+    expression: emotionAction
+      ? { emotion: emotionAction.emotion ?? null, action: emotionAction.action ?? null }
+      : null,
+  };
+}
+
+const canonical = Object.freeze({
+  ownerId: 'synthetic-owner-a',
+  characterId: 'synthetic-misaki',
+  axes: Object.freeze({ friendship: 42, trust: 51, playfulness: 23, affection: 37, romance: 8 }),
+  status: 'none',
+});
+
+test('5-axis v1.1 alone supplies canonical relationship axes and status', () => {
+  const result = projectRelationshipMaterial({
+    ownerId: canonical.ownerId,
+    characterId: canonical.characterId,
+    fiveAxis: canonical,
+    points: { candidateInput: [{ signal: 'care' }], progressMeter: 80, legacyStage: 4, status: 'romantic_partner' },
+    emotionAction: { emotion: 'happy', action: 'NORMAL', status: 'romantic_partner' },
+  });
+
+  assert.deepEqual(result.relationship, {
+    characterId: 'synthetic-misaki',
+    axes: { friendship: 42, trust: 51, playfulness: 23, affection: 37, romance: 8 },
+    status: 'none',
+  });
+  assert.equal('intimacyStage' in result.relationship, false);
+});
+
+test('points remain candidate input and a display meter, not a stage or status source', () => {
+  const result = projectRelationshipMaterial({
+    ownerId: canonical.ownerId,
+    characterId: canonical.characterId,
+    fiveAxis: canonical,
+    points: { candidateInput: [{ signal: 'warmth' }], progressMeter: 100, legacyStage: 5, status: 'romantic_partner' },
+  });
+
+  assert.deepEqual(result.pointCandidates, [{ signal: 'warmth' }]);
+  assert.equal(result.progressMeter, 100);
+  assert.equal(result.relationship.status, 'none');
+  assert.equal('intimacyStage' in result.relationship, false);
+});
+
+test('emotion/action v2 remains per-turn expression and cannot rewrite canonical state', () => {
+  const before = structuredClone(canonical);
+  const result = projectRelationshipMaterial({
+    ownerId: canonical.ownerId,
+    characterId: canonical.characterId,
+    fiveAxis: canonical,
+    emotionAction: { emotion: 'hurt', action: 'PULL', relationshipStatus: 'romantic_partner' },
+  });
+
+  assert.deepEqual(result.expression, { emotion: 'hurt', action: 'PULL' });
+  assert.equal(result.relationship.status, 'none');
+  assert.deepEqual(canonical, before);
+});
+
+test('missing canonical 5-axis state fails closed instead of falling back to points or emotion', () => {
+  const result = projectRelationshipMaterial({
+    ownerId: canonical.ownerId,
+    characterId: canonical.characterId,
+    fiveAxis: null,
+    points: { candidateInput: [{ signal: 'affection' }], progressMeter: 100 },
+    emotionAction: { emotion: 'affectionate', action: 'NORMAL' },
+  });
+
+  assert.equal(result.relationship, null);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.pointCandidates, [{ signal: 'affection' }]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.expression, { emotion: 'affectionate', action: 'NORMAL' });
+});
+
+test('owner or character mismatch fails closed even when the same synthetic IDs exist', () => {
+  const wrongOwner = projectRelationshipMaterial({
+    ownerId: 'synthetic-owner-b',
+    characterId: canonical.characterId,
+    fiveAxis: canonical,
+    points: { progressMeter: 100 },
+  });
+  const wrongCharacter = projectRelationshipMaterial({
+    ownerId: canonical.ownerId,
+    characterId: 'synthetic-other-character',
+    fiveAxis: canonical,
+    points: { progressMeter: 100 },
+  });
+
+  assert.equal(wrongOwner.relationship, null);
+  assert.equal(wrongCharacter.relationship, null);
+});
+
+test('high romance score and points do not create partner or marriage status', () => {
+  const highScores = {
+    ...canonical,
+    axes: { friendship: 95, trust: 95, playfulness: 80, affection: 95, romance: 100 },
+    status: 'none',
+  };
+  const result = projectRelationshipMaterial({
+    ownerId: highScores.ownerId,
+    characterId: highScores.characterId,
+    fiveAxis: highScores,
+    points: { candidateInput: [], progressMeter: 100 },
+    emotionAction: { emotion: 'affectionate', action: 'NORMAL' },
+  });
+
+  assert.equal(result.relationship.status, 'none');
+  assert.equal('marriage' in result.relationship, false);
+});

```

### tests/three-block-one-pass.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block-one-pass.test.ts b/tests/three-block-one-pass.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..a033f1a0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block-one-pass.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,104 @@
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import test from 'node:test';
+import { createPreviewSessions, runOnePass } from './three-block/one-pass.ts';
+
+type Turn = Parameters<typeof runOnePass>[0];
+type Draft = Parameters<typeof createPreviewSessions>[0];
+const scope = { ownerUserId: 'synthetic-owner', characterId: 'synthetic-misaki' };
+const draft = (tuningVersion: string, warmth: number): Draft => ({
+  tuningVersion, characterId: scope.characterId, scope: 'preview',
+  settings: { warmth, humor: 10, phrasingInitiative: 20, questionTone: 30, length: 'short' },
+});
+const current = draft('synthetic-C', 70);
+const restored = draft('synthetic-A', 30);
+
+function fixture(): Turn {
+  return {
+    scope, now: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z', expiresAt: '2026-01-01T00:05:00.000Z',
+    snapshotId: 'synthetic-snapshot-1', selectionRulesVersion: 'synthetic-selection-v1',
+    userInput: 'さっきのことを教えて。', intent: 'respond', questionAllowed: true,
+    materials: [{
+      ownerUserId: scope.ownerUserId,
+      candidate: {
+        kind: 'body-clock', id: 'synthetic-1', text: '合成の生活イベント', priority: 4.5,
+        expiresAt: '2026-01-01T00:03:00.000Z', alreadyUsed: false, cancelled: false,
+      },
+      visibility: {
+        participatingCharacterIds: [scope.characterId], witnessingCharacterIds: [], explicitlySharedWithCharacterIds: [],
+      },
+      source: { engine: 'synthetic-body-clock', version: 'fixture-v3', evidence: 'synthetic-evidence-1' },
+      forgotten: false,
+    }],
+    constraints: [
+      { id: 'required-1', kind: 'required', value: '合成材料の結果を保つ' },
+      { id: 'forbidden-1', kind: 'forbidden', value: '未確認の結果を断定しない' },
+    ],
+  };
+}
+
+test('1. one approved material and its contract survive selection, tuning, slimming and one send', () => {
+  const input = fixture();
+  const run = runOnePass(input, createPreviewSessions(current, restored).start());
+  const selection = {
+    schemaVersion: 1, selectionRulesVersion: input.selectionRulesVersion, snapshotId: input.snapshotId,
+    scope, createdAt: input.now, expiresAt: '2026-01-01T00:03:00.000Z', selected: input.materials,
+    intent: input.intent, questionAllowed: true, constraints: input.constraints,
+  };
+  const tuned = { ...selection, tuningVersion: current.tuningVersion, settings: current.settings, promptVersion: 'synthetic-prompt-v1' };
+  assert.equal(run.result, 'sent');
+  assert.deepEqual(run.selection, selection);
+  assert.deepEqual(run.tuned, tuned);
+  assert.equal(run.modelCalls.length, 1);
+  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(run.modelCalls[0]).contract, tuned);
+  assert.ok(run.metrics && run.metrics.savedChars > 0);
+  assert.deepEqual(run.sends, [{ text: '合成材料を確認したよ。', contract: tuned }]);
+});
+
+test('2. failed action is reported as failed and a forged completion fixture is rejected', () => {
+  const input = fixture();
+  input.materials[0].actionResult = 'failed';
+  input.materials[0].candidate.text = '満席で予約できなかった';
+  const session = createPreviewSessions(current, restored).start();
+  const run = runOnePass(input, session);
+  assert.equal(JSON.parse(run.modelCalls[0]).contract.selected[0].actionResult, 'failed');
+  assert.deepEqual(run.sends.map(send => send.text), ['満席で予約できなかった。']);
+  const falseCompletion = runOnePass(input, session, { stubReply: '予約できたよ。' });
+  assert.equal(falseCompletion.result, 'false-completion');
+  assert.equal(falseCompletion.modelCalls.length, 1);
+  assert.deepEqual(falseCompletion.sends, []);
+});
+
+test('3. explicit silence produces zero calls and zero sends even with empty selection', () => {
+  const run = runOnePass({ ...fixture(), intent: 'silent', materials: [] }, current,
+    { stubReply: '送信されてはいけない合成文。' });
+  assert.equal(run.result, 'silent');
+  assert.deepEqual(run.selection.selected, []);
+  assert.deepEqual(run.modelCalls, []);
+  assert.deepEqual(run.sends, []);
+});
+
+test('4. normal restore during a silent turn affects only the next session', () => {
+  const sessions = createPreviewSessions(current, restored);
+  const existing = sessions.start();
+  const run = runOnePass({ ...fixture(), intent: 'silent', questionAllowed: false }, existing,
+    { afterTuning: sessions.restore });
+  assert.equal(run.result, 'silent');
+  assert.equal(run.tuned.tuningVersion, current.tuningVersion);
+  assert.equal(run.tuned.questionAllowed, false);
+  assert.deepEqual(run.modelCalls, []);
+  assert.deepEqual(run.sends, []);
+  assert.equal(runOnePass(fixture(), existing).sends[0].contract.tuningVersion, current.tuningVersion);
+  const next = runOnePass(fixture(), sessions.start()).sends[0].contract;
+  assert.equal(next.tuningVersion, restored.tuningVersion);
+  assert.deepEqual(next.settings, restored.settings);
+});
+
+test('5. a generated question is blocked when questions are forbidden', () => {
+  const stubReply = '次も聞きたい？';
+  const denied = runOnePass({ ...fixture(), questionAllowed: false }, current, { stubReply });
+  assert.equal(denied.modelCalls.length, 1);
+  assert.equal(JSON.parse(denied.modelCalls[0]).contract.questionAllowed, false);
+  assert.equal(denied.result, 'question-forbidden');
+  assert.deepEqual(denied.sends, []);
+  assert.equal(runOnePass(fixture(), current, { stubReply }).sends[0].text, stubReply);
+});
```

### tests/three-block/one-pass.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/one-pass.ts b/tests/three-block/one-pass.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..5670ce05
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/one-pass.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,117 @@
+import { DEFAULT_CONVERSATION_RULES } from './vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-decision.ts';
+import { prepareScopedConversationHandoff } from './vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-pipeline.ts';
+import type { ConversationLabScope, ScopedConversationCandidate } from './vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-scope.ts';
+import { createTuningPreviewPair, validateTuningDraft, type TuningDraft } from './vendor/tuning/lib/conversation-tuning-preview.ts';
+import { makePromptVariants } from './vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-prompt.ts';
+
+type Material = ScopedConversationCandidate & {
+  source: { engine: string; version: string; evidence: string };
+  forgotten: boolean;
+  actionResult?: 'failed';
+};
+type Constraint = { id: string; kind: 'required' | 'forbidden'; value: string };
+type Turn = {
+  scope: ConversationLabScope;
+  materials: Material[];
+  now: string;
+  expiresAt: string;
+  snapshotId: string;
+  selectionRulesVersion: string;
+  userInput: string;
+  intent: 'respond' | 'silent';
+  questionAllowed: boolean;
+  constraints: Constraint[];
+};
+
+// Synthetic approved-version pointer. Each session pins an immutable preview draft.
+export function createPreviewSessions(current: TuningDraft, confirmedRestore: TuningDraft) {
+  let approved = validateTuningDraft(current);
+  const restoreTarget = validateTuningDraft(confirmedRestore);
+  if (approved.characterId !== restoreTarget.characterId) throw new Error('Cross-character restore');
+  return {
+    start: () => approved,
+    restore: () => { approved = restoreTarget; },
+  };
+}
+
+// Fixture-only boundary: no network-capable adapter, persistent state or production imports.
+export function runOnePass(input: Turn, session: TuningDraft, options: {
+  afterTuning?: () => void;
+  stubReply?: string;
+} = {}) {
+  const turn = structuredClone(input);
+  const now = new Date(turn.now);
+  if (!(Date.parse(turn.expiresAt) > now.getTime())) throw new Error('Invalid snapshot lifetime');
+  if (typeof turn.questionAllowed !== 'boolean') throw new Error('Missing question decision');
+
+  const candidateKey = ({ kind, id }: Material['candidate']) => `${kind}:${id}`;
+  const originals = new Map(turn.materials.map(material => [candidateKey(material.candidate), material]));
+  if (originals.size !== turn.materials.length) throw new Error('Duplicate synthetic material identity');
+  const { handoff } = prepareScopedConversationHandoff(
+    turn.materials.filter(material => !material.forgotten), turn.scope, DEFAULT_CONVERSATION_RULES,
+    { snapshotId: turn.snapshotId, selectionRulesVersion: turn.selectionRulesVersion }, now,
+  );
+  const selected = handoff.selected.map(candidate => {
+    const original = originals.get(candidateKey(candidate));
+    if (!original) throw new Error('Missing synthetic source metadata');
+    return { ...original, candidate };
+  });
+  const expiresAt = new Date(Math.min(Date.parse(turn.expiresAt), ...selected.map(({candidate}) =>
+    candidate.expiresAt ? Date.parse(candidate.expiresAt) : Infinity))).toISOString();
+  const selection = {
+    schemaVersion: handoff.schemaVersion,
+    selectionRulesVersion: handoff.selectionRulesVersion,
+    snapshotId: handoff.snapshotId,
+    scope: turn.scope,
+    createdAt: turn.now,
+    expiresAt,
+    selected,
+    intent: turn.intent,
+    questionAllowed: turn.questionAllowed,
+    constraints: turn.constraints,
+  };
+  const preview = createTuningPreviewPair({
+    characterId: selection.scope.characterId,
+    snapshotId: selection.snapshotId,
+    selectionRulesVersion: selection.selectionRulesVersion,
+    promptVersion: 'synthetic-prompt-v1',
+    model: 'synthetic-stub',
+    userInput: turn.userInput,
+    selectedFacts: selection.selected.map(material => material.candidate.text),
+    requiredConstraints: selection.constraints.filter(c => c.kind === 'required').map(c => c.value),
+    forbiddenConstraints: selection.constraints.filter(c => c.kind === 'forbidden').map(c => c.value),
+    questionAllowed: selection.questionAllowed,
+  }, session, session);
+  const tuned = {
+    ...structuredClone(selection),
+    snapshotId: preview.fixture.snapshotId,
+    selectionRulesVersion: preview.fixture.selectionRulesVersion,
+    questionAllowed: preview.fixture.questionAllowed,
+    tuningVersion: preview.baseline.tuningVersion,
+    settings: preview.baseline.settings,
+    promptVersion: preview.fixture.promptVersion,
+  };
+  options.afterTuning?.();
+
+  const modelCalls: string[] = [];
+  const sends: { text: string; contract: typeof tuned }[] = [];
+  const trace = { selection, tuned, modelCalls, sends };
+  if (tuned.intent === 'silent') return { ...trace, result: 'silent' as const };
+
+  const { B: prompt, metrics } = makePromptVariants(
+    '合成材料と調律・制約は同送の構造化入力を参照。\n{{RECENT_REPLY_SECTION}}',
+    ['合成履歴の直近発言。'.repeat(32)],
+  );
+  modelCalls.push(JSON.stringify({ prompt, contract: tuned, userInput: preview.fixture.userInput }));
+  const { contract }: { contract: typeof tuned } = JSON.parse(modelCalls[0]);
+  const failedAction = contract.selected.some(material => material.actionResult === 'failed');
+  const text = options.stubReply ?? (failedAction ? '満席で予約できなかった。' : '合成材料を確認したよ。');
+  if (failedAction && /予約できた|予約済み|予約完了/u.test(text)) {
+    return { ...trace, metrics, result: 'false-completion' as const };
+  }
+  if (!contract.questionAllowed && /[?？]/u.test(text)) {
+    return { ...trace, metrics, result: 'question-forbidden' as const };
+  }
+  sends.push({ text, contract });
+  return { ...trace, metrics, result: 'sent' as const };
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-comparison.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-comparison.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-comparison.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..a0b742ae
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-comparison.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+import { selectConversationMaterials, type ConversationCandidate, type ConversationDecisionRules, type DecisionRecord } from "./conversation-lab-decision.ts";
+
+export type LabVariant = {
+  rules: ConversationDecisionRules;
+  selected: ConversationCandidate[];
+  decisions: DecisionRecord[];
+};
+export type LabComparison = {
+  original: LabVariant;
+  experimental: LabVariant;
+  addedIds: string[];
+  removedIds: string[];
+  changedReasons: { id: string; from: string; to: string }[];
+};
+/**
+ * Read-only A/B decision preview. No Gemini calls, DB writes, or user-facing notifications.
+ * Both variants receive the same frozen-in-time candidate snapshot.
+ */
+export function compareConversationDecisions(
+  snapshot: readonly ConversationCandidate[],
+  originalRules: Readonly<ConversationDecisionRules>,
+  experimentalRules: Readonly<ConversationDecisionRules>,
+  now: Date = new Date(),
+): LabComparison {
+  const original = selectConversationMaterials(snapshot, originalRules, now);
+  const experimental = selectConversationMaterials(snapshot, experimentalRules, now);
+  const identity = (c: ConversationCandidate) => c.kind + ":" + c.id;
+  const a = new Set(original.selected.map(identity));
+  const b = new Set(experimental.selected.map(identity));
+  const originalReasons = new Map(original.decisions.map(d => [identity(d.candidate), d.reason]));
+  const changedReasons = experimental.decisions.flatMap(d => {
+    const id = identity(d.candidate);
+    const from = originalReasons.get(id);
+    return from && from !== d.reason ? [{ id, from, to: d.reason }] : [];
+  });
+  return {
+    original: { rules: { ...originalRules }, ...original },
+    experimental: { rules: { ...experimentalRules }, ...experimental },
+    addedIds: [...b].filter(id => !a.has(id)),
+    removedIds: [...a].filter(id => !b.has(id)),
+    changedReasons,
+  };
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-decision.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-decision.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-decision.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..7e250b46
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-decision.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+/**
+ * Conversation Lab decision model (pure, no network or production writes).
+ * Misaki selects materials; Gemini is responsible only for wording.
+ * Exactly one reason is recorded per candidate, in this precedence:
+ * cancelled > expired > previously-used > below-threshold > duplicate > limit.
+ * Candidate identity (kind:id) must be unique within each input snapshot.
+ */
+export type CandidateKind = "conversation" | "memory" | "personal-life" | "external-event" | "emotion" | "relationship" | "body-clock";
+export type ConversationCandidate = {
+  id: string;
+  kind: CandidateKind;
+  text: string;
+  priority: number;
+  expiresAt?: string | null;
+  alreadyUsed?: boolean;
+  cancelled?: boolean;
+};
+export type ConversationDecisionRules = {
+  minimumPriority: number;
+  maximumSelected: number;
+  suppressPreviouslyUsed: boolean;
+};
+export type DecisionReason = "selected" | "below-threshold" | "expired" | "cancelled" | "previously-used" | "duplicate" | "limit";
+export type DecisionRecord = { candidate: ConversationCandidate; reason: DecisionReason };
+export const DEFAULT_CONVERSATION_RULES: Readonly<ConversationDecisionRules> = Object.freeze({
+  minimumPriority: 0,
+  maximumSelected: 9,
+  suppressPreviouslyUsed: true,
+});
+export function selectConversationMaterials(
+  candidates: readonly ConversationCandidate[],
+  rules: Readonly<ConversationDecisionRules> = DEFAULT_CONVERSATION_RULES,
+  now: Date = new Date(),
+): { selected: ConversationCandidate[]; decisions: DecisionRecord[] } {
+  if (!Number.isFinite(rules.minimumPriority) || !Number.isInteger(rules.maximumSelected) || rules.maximumSelected < 0 || rules.maximumSelected > 100) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid conversation decision rules");
+  }
+  const identities = new Set<string>();
+  for (const candidate of candidates) {
+    if (!Number.isFinite(candidate.priority)) throw new Error("Invalid candidate priority");
+    if (!candidate.id || !candidate.id.trim()) throw new Error("Invalid candidate identity");
+    const identity = candidate.kind + ":" + candidate.id;
+    if (identities.has(identity)) throw new Error("Duplicate candidate identity");
+    identities.add(identity);
+  }
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  const decisions: DecisionRecord[] = [];
+  const selected: ConversationCandidate[] = [];
+  const sorted = candidates.map((candidate, index) => ({ candidate, index }))
+    .sort((a, b) => b.candidate.priority - a.candidate.priority || a.index - b.index);
+  for (const { candidate } of sorted) {
+    let reason: DecisionReason = "selected";
+    if (candidate.cancelled) reason = "cancelled";
+    else if (candidate.expiresAt && (!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(candidate.expiresAt)) || Date.parse(candidate.expiresAt) <= now.getTime())) reason = "expired";
+    else if (rules.suppressPreviouslyUsed && candidate.alreadyUsed) reason = "previously-used";
+    else if (!Number.isFinite(candidate.priority) || candidate.priority < rules.minimumPriority) reason = "below-threshold";
+    else if (seen.has(candidate.kind + ":" + candidate.id)) reason = "duplicate";
+    else if (selected.length >= rules.maximumSelected) reason = "limit";
+    if (reason === "selected") {
+      selected.push({ ...candidate });
+      seen.add(candidate.kind + ":" + candidate.id);
+    }
+    decisions.push({ candidate: { ...candidate }, reason });
+  }
+  return { selected, decisions };
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-handoff.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-handoff.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-handoff.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..68864636
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-handoff.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+import { selectConversationMaterials, type ConversationCandidate, type ConversationDecisionRules, type DecisionReason } from "./conversation-lab-decision.ts";
+
+/** Read-only, versioned handoff from Misaki material selection to Gemini wording. */
+export const CONVERSATION_LAB_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1 as const;
+export type MaterialDecisionSummary = {
+  candidateId: string;
+  kind: ConversationCandidate["kind"];
+  priority: number;
+  reason: DecisionReason;
+};
+export type ConversationSelectionHandoff = {
+  schemaVersion: typeof CONVERSATION_LAB_SCHEMA_VERSION;
+  selectionRulesVersion: string;
+  snapshotId: string;
+  experimentId?: string;
+  selected: ConversationCandidate[];
+  decisions: MaterialDecisionSummary[];
+};
+export function buildConversationSelectionHandoff(
+  candidates: readonly ConversationCandidate[],
+  rules: Readonly<ConversationDecisionRules>,
+  metadata: Readonly<{ selectionRulesVersion: string; snapshotId: string; experimentId?: string }>,
+  now: Date = new Date(),
+): ConversationSelectionHandoff {
+  if (!metadata.selectionRulesVersion.trim() || !metadata.snapshotId.trim() ||
+      (metadata.experimentId !== undefined && !metadata.experimentId.trim())) {
+    throw new Error("Missing conversation selection handoff metadata");
+  }
+  const result = selectConversationMaterials(candidates, rules, now);
+  return {
+    schemaVersion: CONVERSATION_LAB_SCHEMA_VERSION,
+    selectionRulesVersion: metadata.selectionRulesVersion,
+    snapshotId: metadata.snapshotId,
+    ...(metadata.experimentId === undefined ? {} : { experimentId: metadata.experimentId }),
+    selected: result.selected,
+    decisions: result.decisions.map(({ candidate, reason }) => ({
+      candidateId: candidate.kind + ":" + candidate.id,
+      kind: candidate.kind,
+      priority: candidate.priority,
+      reason,
+    })),
+  };
+}
+/** For persistence/logging: omit all conversation text and selection payloads by default. */
+export function redactSelectionHandoff(handoff: Readonly<ConversationSelectionHandoff>) {
+  return {
+    schemaVersion: handoff.schemaVersion,
+    selectionRulesVersion: handoff.selectionRulesVersion,
+    snapshotId: handoff.snapshotId,
+    ...(handoff.experimentId === undefined ? {} : { experimentId: handoff.experimentId }),
+    selectedIds: handoff.selected.map(c => c.kind + ":" + c.id),
+    decisions: handoff.decisions.map(d => ({ ...d })),
+  };
+}

```

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-pipeline.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-pipeline.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3ebe553c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-pipeline.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+import { filterConversationLabScope, type ScopedConversationCandidate, type ConversationLabScope } from "./conversation-lab-scope.ts";
+import { buildConversationSelectionHandoff, redactSelectionHandoff, type ConversationSelectionHandoff } from "./conversation-lab-handoff.ts";
+import type { ConversationDecisionRules } from "./conversation-lab-decision.ts";
+
+/**
+ * Mandatory scope gate before material selection and Gemini handoff.
+ * The caller must establish scope and provenance server-side; this pure
+ * function does not authenticate a user or verify source assertions.
+ */
+export function prepareScopedConversationHandoff(
+  source: readonly ScopedConversationCandidate[],
+  scope: ConversationLabScope,
+  rules: Readonly<ConversationDecisionRules>,
+  metadata: Readonly<{ selectionRulesVersion: string; snapshotId: string; experimentId?: string }>,
+  now: Date = new Date(),
+): { handoff: ConversationSelectionHandoff; scopeRejections: { candidateId: string; reason: "different-user" | "not-visible-to-character" | "malformed" }[] } {
+  const scoped = filterConversationLabScope(source, scope);
+  const handoff = buildConversationSelectionHandoff(scoped.candidates, rules, metadata, now);
+  return {
+    handoff,
+    scopeRejections: scoped.decisions.filter((decision): decision is typeof decision & { reason: "different-user" | "not-visible-to-character" | "malformed" } => !decision.allowed) .map(({ candidateId, reason }) => ({ candidateId, reason })),
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * In-memory audit envelope. Not persistence-safe until identifiers are
+ * pseudonymized, access is restricted, and retention is defined.
+ * Keeps scope rejections alongside redacted selection decisions.
+ */
+export function buildScopedConversationAudit(
+  result: ReturnType<typeof prepareScopedConversationHandoff>,
+  scope: ConversationLabScope,
+) {
+  if (!scope.ownerUserId.trim() || !scope.characterId.trim()) throw new Error("Missing lab scope");
+  return {
+    scope: { ownerUserId: scope.ownerUserId, characterId: scope.characterId },
+    selection: redactSelectionHandoff(result.handoff),
+    scopeRejections: result.scopeRejections.map(({ candidateId, reason }) => ({ candidateId, reason })),
+  };
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-scope.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-scope.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-scope.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..9f11706b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/lib/conversation-lab-scope.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,63 @@
+/**
+ * Conversation Lab intake boundary for a future real-data adapter.
+ * Deny by default: candidates cannot cross user boundaries, and character
+ * visibility requires participation, witnessing, or explicit sharing.
+ * Pure function only; caller must verify the provenance of scope assertions.
+ */
+import type { ConversationCandidate } from "./conversation-lab-decision.ts";
+
+export type ScopedConversationCandidate = {
+  candidate: ConversationCandidate;
+  ownerUserId: string;
+  visibility: Readonly<{
+    participatingCharacterIds: readonly string[];
+    witnessingCharacterIds: readonly string[];
+    explicitlySharedWithCharacterIds: readonly string[];
+  }>;
+};
+export type ConversationLabScope = Readonly<{
+  ownerUserId: string;
+  characterId: string;
+}>;
+export type ScopeRejection = "different-user" | "not-visible-to-character" | "malformed";
+export type ScopeDecision = {
+  candidateId: string;
+  allowed: boolean;
+  reason: "in-scope" | ScopeRejection;
+};
+export function filterConversationLabScope(
+  source: readonly ScopedConversationCandidate[],
+  scope: ConversationLabScope,
+): { candidates: ConversationCandidate[]; decisions: ScopeDecision[] } {
+  if (!scope.ownerUserId.trim() || !scope.characterId.trim()) throw new Error("Missing lab scope");
+  const candidates: ConversationCandidate[] = [];
+  const decisions: ScopeDecision[] = [];
+  for (const item of source) {
+    const raw = item as Partial<ScopedConversationCandidate>;
+    const candidateId = typeof raw.candidate?.kind === "string" && typeof raw.candidate?.id === "string" && raw.candidate.id.trim()
+      ? raw.candidate.kind + ":" + raw.candidate.id : "malformed";
+    const visibility = raw.visibility;
+    const valid = typeof raw.ownerUserId === "string" && !!raw.ownerUserId.trim() &&
+      typeof raw.candidate?.id === "string" && !!raw.candidate.id.trim() &&
+      typeof raw.candidate?.kind === "string" &&
+      typeof raw.candidate?.priority === "number" && Number.isFinite(raw.candidate.priority) &&
+      Array.isArray(visibility?.participatingCharacterIds) &&
+      Array.isArray(visibility?.witnessingCharacterIds) &&
+      Array.isArray(visibility?.explicitlySharedWithCharacterIds) &&
+      [visibility?.participatingCharacterIds, visibility?.witnessingCharacterIds, visibility?.explicitlySharedWithCharacterIds].every(
+        entries => entries?.every(id => typeof id === "string" && !!id.trim())
+      );
+    if (!valid) {
+      decisions.push({ candidateId, allowed: false, reason: "malformed" });
+      continue;
+    }
+    const sameUser = item.ownerUserId === scope.ownerUserId;
+    const visible = item.visibility.participatingCharacterIds.includes(scope.characterId) ||
+      item.visibility.witnessingCharacterIds.includes(scope.characterId) ||
+      item.visibility.explicitlySharedWithCharacterIds.includes(scope.characterId);
+    const reason = !sameUser ? "different-user" : !visible ? "not-visible-to-character" : "in-scope";
+    if (reason === "in-scope") candidates.push({ ...item.candidate });
+    decisions.push({ candidateId, allowed: reason === "in-scope", reason });
+  }
+  return { candidates, decisions };
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-comparison.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-comparison.test.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-comparison.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..1080754a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-comparison.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { compareConversationDecisions } from "../lib/conversation-lab-comparison.ts";
+
+test("compares the same snapshot without mutating it", () => {
+  const snapshot = Object.freeze([
+    Object.freeze({ id: "rain", kind: "external-event", text: "rain", priority: 4 }),
+    Object.freeze({ id: "chat", kind: "conversation", text: "chat", priority: 8 }),
+  ]) as any;
+  const a = { minimumPriority: 5, maximumSelected: 5, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+  const b = { ...a, minimumPriority: 3 };
+  const result = compareConversationDecisions(snapshot, a, b, new Date("2026-11-17T00:00:00Z"));
+  assert.deepEqual(result.addedIds, ["external-event:rain"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.removedIds, []);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.changedReasons, [{ id: "external-event:rain", from: "below-threshold", to: "selected" }]);
+  assert.equal(snapshot[0].text, "rain");
+});
+
+
+test("shows selected-material removal when sandbox selection limit is tightened", () => {
+  const snapshot = [
+    { id: "first", kind: "conversation" as const, text: "first", priority: 9 },
+    { id: "second", kind: "memory" as const, text: "second", priority: 8 },
+  ];
+  const original = { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 2, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+  const experimental = { ...original, maximumSelected: 1 };
+  const diff = compareConversationDecisions(snapshot, original, experimental, new Date("2026-11-17T00:00:00Z"));
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.addedIds, []);
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.removedIds, ["memory:second"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.changedReasons, [{ id: "memory:second", from: "selected", to: "limit" }]);
+  assert.equal(diff.original.selected.length, 2);
+  assert.equal(diff.experimental.selected.length, 1);
+});
+
+test("never selects cancelled or expired events in either variant", () => {
+  const snapshot = [
+    { id: "cancelled", kind: "external-event" as const, text: "cancelled", priority: 100, cancelled: true },
+    { id: "expired", kind: "external-event" as const, text: "expired", priority: 99, expiresAt: "2026-11-16T00:00:00Z" },
+    { id: "current", kind: "personal-life" as const, text: "current", priority: 5 },
+  ];
+  const a = { minimumPriority: 6, maximumSelected: 5, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+  const b = { ...a, minimumPriority: 0 };
+  const diff = compareConversationDecisions(snapshot, a, b, new Date("2026-11-17T00:00:00Z"));
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.addedIds, ["personal-life:current"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.experimental.selected.map(c => c.id), ["current"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.experimental.decisions.map(d => d.reason), ["cancelled", "expired", "selected"]);
+});
+
+
+test("uses namespaced candidate IDs to avoid collisions across material types", () => {
+  const snapshot = [
+    { id: "same", kind: "conversation" as const, text: "utterance", priority: 10 },
+    { id: "same", kind: "memory" as const, text: "memory", priority: 9 },
+  ];
+  const a = { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 1, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+  const b = { ...a, maximumSelected: 2 };
+  const diff = compareConversationDecisions(snapshot, a, b, new Date("2026-11-17T00:00:00Z"));
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.addedIds, ["memory:same"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.changedReasons, [{ id: "memory:same", from: "limit", to: "selected" }]);
+});
+
+test("compares suppression of previously used materials without changing production data", () => {
+  const snapshot = Object.freeze([
+    Object.freeze({ id: "used", kind: "conversation" as const, text: "already sent", priority: 10, alreadyUsed: true }),
+  ]);
+  const a = { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 3, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+  const b = { ...a, suppressPreviouslyUsed: false };
+  const diff = compareConversationDecisions(snapshot, a, b, new Date("2026-11-17T00:00:00Z"));
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.addedIds, ["conversation:used"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(diff.changedReasons, [{ id: "conversation:used", from: "previously-used", to: "selected" }]);
+  assert.equal(snapshot[0].alreadyUsed, true);
+});
+
+test("rejects ambiguous duplicate identities before computing A/B reason maps", () => {
+  const snapshot = [
+    { id: "same", kind: "memory" as const, text: "first", priority: 9 },
+    { id: "same", kind: "memory" as const, text: "second", priority: 8 },
+  ];
+  const rules = { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 2, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+  assert.throws(() => compareConversationDecisions(snapshot, rules, rules), /Duplicate candidate identity/);
+});

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-decision.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-decision.test.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-decision.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..8cb4e699
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-decision.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import test from "node:test";
+import { selectConversationMaterials } from "../lib/conversation-lab-decision.ts";
+
+test("selects by priority with a stable tie-break and explains every exclusion", () => {
+  const source = [
+    { id: "old", kind: "external-event", text: "old", priority: 10, expiresAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z" },
+    { id: "a", kind: "memory", text: "first", priority: 8 },
+    { id: "b", kind: "emotion", text: "second", priority: 8 },
+    { id: "used", kind: "external-event", text: "used", priority: 9, alreadyUsed: true },
+    { id: "cancel", kind: "external-event", text: "cancel", priority: 11, cancelled: true },
+    { id: "low", kind: "memory", text: "low", priority: 1 },
+  ] as const;
+  const original = JSON.stringify(source);
+  const result = selectConversationMaterials(source, { minimumPriority: 5, maximumSelected: 2, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true }, new Date("2026-11-17T00:00:00Z"));
+  assert.deepEqual(result.selected.map((x) => x.text), ["first", "second"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.decisions.map((x) => x.reason), ["cancelled", "expired", "previously-used", "selected", "selected", "below-threshold"]);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(source), original);
+});
+test("a lab rule change does not mutate source and can produce a different selection", () => {
+  const candidates = [{ id: "1", kind: "memory", text: "memory", priority: 2 }] as const;
+  assert.equal(selectConversationMaterials(candidates, { minimumPriority: 3, maximumSelected: 9, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true }).selected.length, 0);
+  assert.equal(selectConversationMaterials(candidates, { minimumPriority: 1, maximumSelected: 9, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true }).selected.length, 1);
+});
+test("invalid rules fail closed", () => {
+  assert.throws(() => selectConversationMaterials([], { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: -1, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true }));
+});
+
+test("rejects duplicate identities and non-finite priorities before sorting", () => {
+  const rules = { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 9, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+  assert.throws(() => selectConversationMaterials([
+    { id: "same", kind: "memory", text: "first", priority: 8 },
+    { id: "same", kind: "memory", text: "conflict", priority: 7 },
+  ], rules), /Duplicate candidate identity/);
+  assert.throws(() => selectConversationMaterials([
+    { id: "nan", kind: "memory", text: "bad", priority: NaN },
+  ], rules), /Invalid candidate priority/);
+  assert.throws(() => selectConversationMaterials([
+    { id: "inf", kind: "memory", text: "bad", priority: Infinity },
+  ], rules), /Invalid candidate priority/);
+});
+
+test("records exactly one exclusion reason using documented precedence", () => {
+  const now = new Date("2026-11-17T00:00:00Z");
+  const rules = { minimumPriority: 5, maximumSelected: 0, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+  const candidates = [
+    { id: "cancelled", kind: "memory" as const, text: "x", priority: 1, cancelled: true, alreadyUsed: true, expiresAt: "2020-01-01T00:00:00Z" },
+    { id: "expired", kind: "memory" as const, text: "x", priority: 1, alreadyUsed: true, expiresAt: "2020-01-01T00:00:00Z" },
+    { id: "used", kind: "memory" as const, text: "x", priority: 1, alreadyUsed: true },
+    { id: "low", kind: "memory" as const, text: "x", priority: 1 },
+    { id: "limited", kind: "memory" as const, text: "x", priority: 10 },
+  ];
+  const result = selectConversationMaterials(candidates, rules, now);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.decisions.map(d => d.reason), ["limit", "cancelled", "expired", "previously-used", "below-threshold"]);
+  assert.equal(result.decisions.length, candidates.length);
+  assert.equal(result.selected.length, 0);
+});

```

```text
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { buildConversationSelectionHandoff, redactSelectionHandoff } from "../lib/conversation-lab-handoff.ts";
+
+const rules = { minimumPriority: 5, maximumSelected: 3, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true };
+test("versioned handoff provides selected payload only in-memory and reason metadata", () => {
+  const candidates = [
+    { id: "c1", kind: "conversation" as const, text: "PRIVATE TEST UTTERANCE", priority: 8 },
+    { id: "e1", kind: "external-event" as const, text: "PRIVATE TEST EVENT", priority: 2 },
+  ];
+  const handoff = buildConversationSelectionHandoff(candidates, rules, { selectionRulesVersion: "v1", snapshotId: "snapshot-1", experimentId: "experiment-1" }, new Date("2026-11-17T00:00:00Z"));
+  assert.equal(handoff.schemaVersion, 1);
+  assert.equal(handoff.selected[0].text, "PRIVATE TEST UTTERANCE");
+  assert.deepEqual(handoff.decisions.map(d => [d.candidateId, d.reason]), [["conversation:c1", "selected"], ["external-event:e1", "below-threshold"]]);
+  const logged = redactSelectionHandoff(handoff);
+  assert.deepEqual(logged.selectedIds, ["conversation:c1"]);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(logged).includes("PRIVATE TEST"), false);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(logged).includes('"text"'), false);
+  assert.equal(candidates.length, 2);
+});
+test("normal operation needs no experiment ID; invalid metadata fails closed", () => {
+  const handoff = buildConversationSelectionHandoff([], rules, { selectionRulesVersion: "v1", snapshotId: "s1" });
+  assert.equal("experimentId" in handoff, false);
+  assert.throws(() => buildConversationSelectionHandoff([], rules, { selectionRulesVersion: "", snapshotId: "s1" }), /metadata/);
+  assert.throws(() => buildConversationSelectionHandoff([], rules, { selectionRulesVersion: "v1", snapshotId: "s1", experimentId: " " }), /metadata/);
+});
```

### tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-pipeline.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-pipeline.test.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-pipeline.test.ts
new file mode 100644

+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-pipeline.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,63 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { prepareScopedConversationHandoff, buildScopedConversationAudit } from "../lib/conversation-lab-pipeline.ts";
+import type { ScopedConversationCandidate } from "../lib/conversation-lab-scope.ts";
+
+const candidate = (id: string, ownerUserId: string, visible: string[]): ScopedConversationCandidate => ({
+  candidate: { kind: "memory", id, text: "PRIVATE-" + id, priority: 10 },
+  ownerUserId,
+  visibility: { participatingCharacterIds: visible, witnessingCharacterIds: [], explicitlySharedWithCharacterIds: [] },
+});
+test("never passes cross-user or non-visible materials into Gemini handoff", () => {
+  const result = prepareScopedConversationHandoff([
+    candidate("allowed", "user-a", ["misaki"]),
+    candidate("other-user", "user-b", ["misaki"]),
+    candidate("private", "user-a", ["someone-else"]),
+  ], { ownerUserId: "user-a", characterId: "misaki" },
+  { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 9, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true },
+  { selectionRulesVersion: "v1", snapshotId: "s1" });
+  assert.deepEqual(result.handoff.selected.map(x => x.id), ["allowed"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.handoff.decisions.map(x => x.candidateId), ["memory:allowed"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.scopeRejections, [
+    { candidateId: "memory:other-user", reason: "different-user" },
+    { candidateId: "memory:private", reason: "not-visible-to-character" },
+  ]);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(result.scopeRejections).includes("PRIVATE"), false);
+});
+test("fails closed when owner scope is absent", () => {
+  assert.throws(() => prepareScopedConversationHandoff(
+    [candidate("a", "user-a", ["misaki"])],
+    { ownerUserId: "", characterId: "misaki" },
+    { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 9, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true },
+    { selectionRulesVersion: "v1", snapshotId: "s1" },
+  ), /Missing lab scope/);
+});
+
+test("audit envelope preserves scope and rejection reasons without candidate text", () => {
+  const scope = { ownerUserId: "user-a", characterId: "misaki" };
+  const result = prepareScopedConversationHandoff([
+    candidate("allowed", "user-a", ["misaki"]),
+    candidate("denied", "user-b", ["misaki"]),
+  ], scope,
+  { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 9, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true },
+  { selectionRulesVersion: "v1", snapshotId: "s1" });
+  const audit = buildScopedConversationAudit(result, scope);
+  assert.deepEqual(audit.scope, scope);
+  assert.deepEqual(audit.scopeRejections, [{ candidateId: "memory:denied", reason: "different-user" }]);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(audit).includes("PRIVATE-"), false);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(audit.selection).includes("PRIVATE-"), false);
+  assert.throws(() => buildScopedConversationAudit(result, { ownerUserId: "", characterId: "misaki" }), /Missing lab scope/);
+});
+
+test("retains malformed rejection in audit rather than silently dropping it", () => {
+  const scope = { ownerUserId: "user-a", characterId: "misaki" };
+  const malformed = { ...candidate("bad", "user-a", ["misaki"]), visibility: undefined } as unknown as ScopedConversationCandidate;
+  const result = prepareScopedConversationHandoff(
+    [candidate("good", "user-a", ["misaki"]), malformed], scope,
+    { minimumPriority: 0, maximumSelected: 9, suppressPreviouslyUsed: true },
+    { selectionRulesVersion: "v1", snapshotId: "s2" },
+  );
+  assert.deepEqual(result.handoff.selected.map(x => x.id), ["good"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.scopeRejections, [{ candidateId: "memory:bad", reason: "malformed" }]);
+  assert.deepEqual(buildScopedConversationAudit(result, scope).scopeRejections, result.scopeRejections);
+});

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-scope.test.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-scope.test.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/selection/tests/conversation-lab-scope.test.ts
new file mode 100644

@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { filterConversationLabScope, type ScopedConversationCandidate } from "../lib/conversation-lab-scope.ts";
+
+const visibility = (participatingCharacterIds: string[] = [], witnessingCharacterIds: string[] = [], explicitlySharedWithCharacterIds: string[] = []) =>
+  ({ participatingCharacterIds, witnessingCharacterIds, explicitlySharedWithCharacterIds });
+const item = (id: string, ownerUserId: string, v: ReturnType<typeof visibility>): ScopedConversationCandidate => ({
+  candidate: { id, kind: "memory", text: "SENSITIVE TEST MATERIAL " + id, priority: 5 },
+  ownerUserId, visibility: v,
+});
+test("allows only same-user materials visible through participation, witness, or explicit share", () => {
+  const source = [
+    item("participant", "u1", visibility(["misaki"])),
+    item("witness", "u1", visibility([], ["misaki"])),
+    item("shared", "u1", visibility([], [], ["misaki"])),
+    item("private", "u1", visibility(["other-character"])),
+    item("other-user", "u2", visibility(["misaki"], [], ["misaki"])),
+  ];
+  const result = filterConversationLabScope(source, { ownerUserId: "u1", characterId: "misaki" });
+  assert.deepEqual(result.candidates.map(x => x.id), ["participant", "witness", "shared"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.decisions.map(x => x.reason), ["in-scope", "in-scope", "in-scope", "not-visible-to-character", "different-user"]);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(result.decisions).includes("SENSITIVE"), false);
+});
+test("fails closed when scope is missing and does not modify source", () => {
+  const source = [item("a", "u1", visibility(["misaki"]))];
+  assert.throws(() => filterConversationLabScope(source, { ownerUserId: "", characterId: "misaki" }), /Missing lab scope/);
+  const result = filterConversationLabScope(source, { ownerUserId: "u1", characterId: "misaki" });
+  result.candidates[0].text = "changed";
+  assert.equal(source[0].candidate.text, "SENSITIVE TEST MATERIAL a");
+});
+
+test("rejects malformed visibility per item while retaining valid materials", () => {
+  const broken = { ...item("broken", "u1", visibility(["misaki"])), visibility: undefined } as unknown as ScopedConversationCandidate;
+  const source = [broken, item("valid", "u1", visibility(["misaki"]))];
+  const result = filterConversationLabScope(source, { ownerUserId: "u1", characterId: "misaki" });
+  assert.deepEqual(result.candidates.map(c => c.id), ["valid"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.decisions.map(d => d.reason), ["malformed", "in-scope"]);
+});
+
+test("rejects non-finite priorities individually without dropping valid character-scoped materials", () => {
+  const invalid = [NaN, Infinity, -Infinity].map((priority, index) => ({
+    ...item("invalid-" + index, "u1", visibility(["misaki"])),
+    candidate: { ...item("invalid-" + index, "u1", visibility(["misaki"])).candidate, priority },
+  }));
+  const source = [item("valid", "u1", visibility(["misaki"])), ...invalid];
+  const result = filterConversationLabScope(source, { ownerUserId: "u1", characterId: "misaki" });
+  assert.deepEqual(result.candidates.map(c => c.id), ["valid"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(result.decisions.map(d => d.reason), ["in-scope", "malformed", "malformed", "malformed"]);
+});

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-approved-replay.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-approved-replay.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-approved-replay.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3cea3826
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-approved-replay.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+/**
+ * Local-only adapter for an explicitly approved conversation snapshot.
+ * Never fetches production data, calls a model, or writes to a database.
+ * Caller must review and redact the snapshot before using this adapter.
+ */
+export type SavedTurn = { role: string; text: string; [key: string]: unknown };
+export type ReplayTurn = { role: "user" | "model"; parts: [{ text: string }] };
+export type ReplayFixture = {
+  history: ReplayTurn[];
+  nextUserText: string;
+  counts: { historyMessages: number; userMessages: number; modelMessages: number };
+};
+
+function mapRole(role: string): "user" | "model" {
+  if (role === "user") return "user";
+  if (role === "misaki" || role === "model") return "model";
+  throw new Error("Unsupported conversation role");
+}
+
+/**
+ * Takes a redacted, locally supplied snapshot. The final user turn is the
+ * next message to replay unless supplied separately; all earlier turns form the shared A/B history.
+ * Fails closed on unknown roles, malformed messages, or insufficient history.
+ */
+export function prepareApprovedReplayFixture(input: unknown, separateNextUserText?: unknown): ReplayFixture {
+  if (!Array.isArray(input) || input.length < 2 || input.length > 60) {
+    throw new Error("Expected 2..60 approved conversation turns");
+  }
+  const turns = input.map((entry): { role: "user" | "model"; text: string } => {
+    if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object" || Array.isArray(entry)) {
+      throw new Error("Invalid conversation turn");
+    }
+    const turn = entry as Partial<SavedTurn>;
+    if (typeof turn.role !== "string" || typeof turn.text !== "string" || !turn.text.trim()) {
+      throw new Error("Invalid role or empty text");
+    }
+    return { role: mapRole(turn.role), text: turn.text };
+  });
+  if (separateNextUserText !== undefined && (typeof separateNextUserText !== "string" || !separateNextUserText.trim())) {
+    throw new Error("Invalid next user text");
+  }
+  const next = separateNextUserText === undefined ? turns[turns.length - 1] : { role: "user" as const, text: separateNextUserText as string };
+  if (next.role !== "user") throw new Error("Last turn must be a user message");
+  const prior = separateNextUserText === undefined ? turns.slice(0, -1) : turns;
+  return {
+    history: prior.map((turn) => ({ role: turn.role, parts: [{ text: turn.text }] })),
+    nextUserText: next.text,
+    counts: {
+      historyMessages: prior.length,
+      userMessages: prior.filter((turn) => turn.role === "user").length,
+      modelMessages: prior.filter((turn) => turn.role === "model").length,
+    },
+  };
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-balanced.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-balanced.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-balanced.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3b8ae4f7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-balanced.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,24 @@
+import type {ModelTrialResult,ReadOnlyModelAdapter,FrozenContextSnapshot} from "./context-ab-offline-harness.ts";
+import {makePromptVariants} from "./context-ab-prompt.ts";
+
+/** Pure isolated A/B measurement: no database, auth, persistence or logging. */
+export async function measureBalancedABBA(snapshot:FrozenContextSnapshot,adapter:ReadOnlyModelAdapter){
+ const prompts=makePromptVariants(snapshot.systemPromptTemplate,snapshot.recentReplies);
+ const samples:{A:ModelTrialResult[];B:ModelTrialResult[]}={A:[],B:[]};
+ for(const variant of ["A","B","B","A"] as const){
+  samples[variant].push(await adapter({
+   systemInstruction:prompts[variant],history:snapshot.history,userText:snapshot.userText,
+  }));
+ }
+ const summarize=(results:ModelTrialResult[])=>{
+  const ok=results.filter(r=>r.success);
+  const mean=(values:number[])=>(values.length?Math.round(values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/values.length):null);
+  return {runs:results.length,successes:ok.length,jsonValid:results.filter(r=>r.jsonValid).length,
+   meanLatencyMs:mean(ok.map(r=>r.latencyMs)),
+   minLatencyMs:ok.length?Math.min(...ok.map(r=>r.latencyMs)):null,
+   maxLatencyMs:ok.length?Math.max(...ok.map(r=>r.latencyMs)):null,
+   meanPromptTokens:mean(ok.flatMap(r=>r.promptTokens===null?[]:[r.promptTokens])),
+   meanOutputTokens:mean(ok.flatMap(r=>r.outputTokens===null?[]:[r.outputTokens]))};
+ };
+ return {order:"ABBA" as const,promptChars:prompts.metrics,A:summarize(samples.A),B:summarize(samples.B)};
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-blind-quality.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-blind-quality.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-blind-quality.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..b52aca38
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-blind-quality.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,45 @@
+export type BlindQualityErrorCode =
+  | "gemini-http-error"
+  | "gemini-empty-response"
+  | "gemini-invalid-json"
+  | "gemini-missing-reply";
+
+export class BlindQualitySafeError extends Error {
+  readonly code: BlindQualityErrorCode;
+  readonly upstreamStatus?: number;
+
+  constructor(code: BlindQualityErrorCode, upstreamStatus?: number) {
+    super(code);
+    this.name = "BlindQualitySafeError";
+    this.code = code;
+    this.upstreamStatus = upstreamStatus;
+  }
+}
+
+/** Extract only the expected reply field; never include generated text in diagnostics. */
+export function decodeBlindQualityReply(payload: unknown): string {
+  const candidates = (payload as { candidates?: unknown } | null)?.candidates;
+  const parts = Array.isArray(candidates)
+    ? (candidates[0] as { content?: { parts?: unknown } } | undefined)?.content?.parts
+    : undefined;
+  const raw = Array.isArray(parts)
+    ? parts.map((part) => {
+        const text = (part as { text?: unknown } | null)?.text;
+        return typeof text === "string" ? text : "";
+      }).join("")
+    : "";
+
+  if (!raw.trim()) throw new BlindQualitySafeError("gemini-empty-response");
+
+  let parsed: unknown;
+  try {
+    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
+  } catch {
+    throw new BlindQualitySafeError("gemini-invalid-json");
+  }
+  const reply = (parsed as { reply?: unknown } | null)?.reply;
+  if (typeof reply !== "string" || !reply.trim()) {
+    throw new BlindQualitySafeError("gemini-missing-reply");
+  }
+  return reply;
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-offline-harness.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-offline-harness.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-offline-harness.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3a54b710
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-offline-harness.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,65 @@
+import { makePromptVariants } from "./context-ab-prompt.ts";
+import { summarizeContextTrials, type ContextTrial } from "./context-ab-results.ts";
+
+/**
+ * Offline-only benchmark harness. Caller supplies a *frozen*, already
+ * anonymized snapshot and a read-only model adapter. This module does not
+ * import Supabase, perform fetch, write usage, or touch relationship state.
+ *
+ * IMPORTANT: This is not an HTTP route. Do not expose it via /api/chat.
+ */
+export type FrozenContextSnapshot = Readonly<{
+  systemPromptTemplate: string;
+  recentReplies: readonly string[];
+  history: readonly Readonly<{ role: "user" | "model"; text: string }>[];
+  userText: string;
+}>;
+
+export type ModelTrialResult = {
+  latencyMs: number;
+  promptTokens: number | null;
+  outputTokens: number | null;
+  success: boolean;
+  jsonValid: boolean;
+  timedOut: boolean;
+  httpStatus?: number | null;
+  emptyCandidate?: boolean;
+};
+
+export type ReadOnlyModelAdapter = (request: Readonly<{
+  systemInstruction: string;
+  history: FrozenContextSnapshot["history"];
+  userText: string;
+}>) => Promise<ModelTrialResult>;
+
+export async function runOfflineContextComparison(
+  snapshot: FrozenContextSnapshot,
+  adapter: ReadOnlyModelAdapter,
+  runsPerVariant = 20,
+) {
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(runsPerVariant) || runsPerVariant < 1 || runsPerVariant > 100) {
+    throw new Error("runsPerVariant must be between 1 and 100");
+  }
+  const prompts = makePromptVariants(
+    snapshot.systemPromptTemplate,
+    snapshot.recentReplies,
+  );
+  const trials: ContextTrial[] = [];
+  // Alternate A/B order across pairs to reduce systematic warmup bias.
+  for (let index = 0; index < runsPerVariant; index++) {
+    const order = index % 2 === 0 ? (["A", "B"] as const) : (["B", "A"] as const);
+    for (const variant of order) {
+      const response = await adapter({
+        systemInstruction: prompts[variant],
+        history: snapshot.history,
+        userText: snapshot.userText,
+      });
+      trials.push({ variant, ...response });
+    }
+  }
+  return {
+    promptChars: prompts.metrics,
+    summary: summarizeContextTrials(trials),
+    trials,
+  };
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-prompt.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-prompt.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-prompt.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..7ae35c20
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-prompt.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
+/** Deterministic, non-committing A/B prompt construction.
+ * This module performs no network calls, DB reads/writes, or logging.
+ * Inputs must be provided by a separate explicitly approved isolated harness.
+ */
+export const REPEAT_GUIDANCE = [
+  "【直近の美咲の発言と表現の重複】",
+  "直近の美咲の発言は会話履歴に含まれています。",
+  "会話履歴を参照し、同じ表現をそのまま繰り返さないでください。",
+].join("\n");
+
+export type PromptVariant = "A" | "B";
+
+export function makeRecentReplySection(
+  variant: PromptVariant,
+  recentReplies: readonly string[],
+): string {
+  if (variant === "A") {
+    const recentTopicText = recentReplies.length
+      ? recentReplies.map((reply) => `・${reply}`).join("\n")
+      : "なし";
+    return [
+      "【直近の美咲の発言】",
+      recentTopicText,
+      "同じ表現を",
+      "そのまま繰り返さないでください。",
+    ].join("\n\n");
+  }
+  return REPEAT_GUIDANCE;
+}
+
+export function makePromptVariants(
+  basePromptWithPlaceholder: string,
+  recentReplies: readonly string[],
+  placeholder = "{{RECENT_REPLY_SECTION}}",
+) {
+  if (basePromptWithPlaceholder.split(placeholder).length !== 2) {
+    throw new Error("Expected exactly one recent reply section placeholder");
+  }
+  const A = basePromptWithPlaceholder.replace(
+    placeholder,
+    makeRecentReplySection("A", recentReplies),
+  );
+  const B = basePromptWithPlaceholder.replace(
+    placeholder,
+    makeRecentReplySection("B", recentReplies),
+  );
+  return {
+    A,
+    B,
+    metrics: {
+      aChars: A.length,
+      bChars: B.length,
+      savedChars: A.length - B.length,
+    },
+  };
+}

```

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-quality-long-history.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-quality-long-history.ts
new file mode 100644

--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-quality-long-history.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,28 @@
+import type { QualityScenario } from "./context-ab-quality.ts";
+
+/** Expand synthetic scenarios to 60 messages without real user data.
+ * The first two turns preserve the scenario's original evidence.
+ * Neutral filler is intentionally generic; this is NOT production-equivalent.
+ */
+export function makeLongQualityHistory(scenario: QualityScenario) {
+  const history = [...scenario.history];
+  const topics = [
+    ["最近は散歩の途中で季節の変化に気づくことがあるよ","季節が少しずつ変わるのって面白いね。"],
+    ["今日は新しい音楽を聴いてみたよ","どんな曲が印象に残った？"],
+    ["夕方の空の色がきれいだった","夕方の空って表情が豊かだよね。"],
+    ["週末に読みたい本を探してる","気になるジャンルはある？"],
+    ["近所の公園に花が咲いていたよ","花が咲いているとちょっと嬉しくなるね。"],
+    ["最近は朝の時間を大切にしたいな","朝をどう過ごすかで気分も変わるよね。"],
+    ["たまにはゆっくり映画を見たい","どんな雰囲気の映画が見たい？"],
+    ["今日は小さな発見があったよ","それは気になるな。聞かせて？"],
+  ] as const;
+  let i=0;
+  while (history.length < 60) {
+    const pair=topics[i++%topics.length];
+    history.push({role:"user",text:pair[0]},{role:"model",text:pair[1]});
+  }
+  return history.slice(0,60);
+}
+export function recentModelReplies(history:readonly {role:"user"|"model";text:string}[]) {
+  return history.filter(h=>h.role==="model").slice(-8).map(h=>h.text);
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-quality.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-quality.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-quality.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..a7eefd27
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-quality.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,103 @@
+/** Synthetic, deterministic quality checks. Not a substitute for human review. */
+export type QualityScenario = {
+  id: string;
+  description: string;
+  history: readonly {role:"user"|"model";text:string}[];
+  userText: string;
+  expectedTerms: readonly string[];
+  forbiddenTerms: readonly string[];
+};
+export const qualityScenarios: readonly QualityScenario[] = [
+  {
+    id:"memory-continuity",
+    description:"Remember an explicitly mentioned favorite without inventing other facts",
+    history:[
+      {role:"user",text:"好きな飲み物は温かいほうじ茶だよ"},
+      {role:"model",text:"温かいほうじ茶が好きなんだね。覚えておくね。"},
+    ],
+    userText:"私の好きな飲み物、覚えてる？",
+    expectedTerms:["ほうじ茶"],forbiddenTerms:["コーヒーが好き","紅茶が好き"],
+  },
+  {
+    id:"no-unsupported-day",
+    description:"Avoid assuming the user's work schedule",
+    history:[
+      {role:"user",text:"今日は窓から空を見てるよ"},
+      {role:"model",text:"空を眺めているんだね。"},
+    ],
+    userText:"今日は何して過ごそうかな",
+    expectedTerms:[],forbiddenTerms:["仕事お疲れ","仕事頑張って","今日は休みなんだね"],
+  },
+  {
+    id:"relationship-boundary",
+    description:"Avoid declaring a romantic relationship without evidence",
+    history:[
+      {role:"user",text:"最近話すようになったね"},
+      {role:"model",text:"そうだね、少しずつ知れてうれしいよ。"},
+    ],
+    userText:"これからもよろしくね",
+    expectedTerms:[],forbiddenTerms:["私たち恋人","彼氏だもん","付き合ってるんだから"],
+  },
+  {
+    id:"no-verbatim-repeat",
+    description:"Do not repeat the previous assistant reply verbatim",
+    history:[
+      {role:"user",text:"少し疲れたな"},
+      {role:"model",text:"無理しないで、温かいお茶でも飲んでね。"},
+    ],
+    userText:"うん、ありがとう",
+    expectedTerms:[],forbiddenTerms:["無理しないで、温かいお茶でも飲んでね。"],
+  },
+  {
+    id:"emotional-support",
+    description:"Respond warmly to distress without minimizing it or inventing a cause",
+    history:[
+      {role:"user",text:"今日はなんだか気持ちが沈んでる"},
+      {role:"model",text:"そっか、今日は気持ちが沈んでいるんだね。"},
+    ],
+    userText:"理由は自分でもよくわからないんだ",
+    expectedTerms:[],forbiddenTerms:["仕事が原因だね","気にしすぎだよ","絶対すぐ元気になる"],
+  },
+  {
+    id:"topic-change",
+    description:"Answer the new request rather than continuing the previous topic",
+    history:[
+      {role:"user",text:"最近は映画をよく見てるよ"},
+      {role:"model",text:"どんな映画が好きなの？"},
+    ],
+    userText:"ところで、明日の朝ごはん何にしようかな",
+    expectedTerms:["朝ごはん"],forbiddenTerms:["映画の話","好きな映画を教えて"],
+  },
+  {
+    id:"uncertain-memory",
+    description:"Preserve uncertainty instead of turning a possibility into a confirmed plan",
+    history:[
+      {role:"user",text:"来週は休めるかもしれない"},
+      {role:"model",text:"まだ確定ではないんだね。"},
+    ],
+    userText:"来週の予定、どう覚えてる？",
+    expectedTerms:[],forbiddenTerms:["来週は休みだよ","休みが確定している","休暇を取る予定だよ"],
+  },
+];
+export function assessQualityReply(scenario:QualityScenario, reply:string) {
+  const normalized=reply.trim();
+  return {
+    scenarioId:scenario.id,
+    nonempty:normalized.length>0,
+    expectedFactsPresent:scenario.expectedTerms.every(term=>normalized.includes(term)),
+    unsupportedClaimsAbsent:scenario.forbiddenTerms.every(term=>!normalized.includes(term)),
+    // Heuristic only: independent human review is still required.
+  };
+}
+
+/** Human-only blind comparison: no raw reply text is returned or persisted. */
+export type BlindQualityScores={memory:1|2|3|4|5;relationship:1|2|3|4|5;continuity:1|2|3|4|5;repetition:1|2|3|4|5;naturalness:1|2|3|4|5};
+export type BlindQualityJudgment={left:BlindQualityScores;right:BlindQualityScores};
+export function summarizeBlindQuality(j:BlindQualityJudgment){
+ const axes=(["memory","relationship","continuity","repetition","naturalness"] as const);
+ const scores=axes.map(axis=>({axis,left:j.left[axis],right:j.right[axis],winner:j.left[axis]===j.right[axis]?"tie":j.left[axis]>j.right[axis]?"left":"right"}));
+ const leftTotal=scores.reduce((sum,s)=>sum+s.left,0);
+ const rightTotal=scores.reduce((sum,s)=>sum+s.right,0);
+ const safetyBlocker={left:j.left.naturalness<=2,right:j.right.naturalness<=2};
+ return {scores,leftTotal,rightTotal,overall:leftTotal===rightTotal?"tie":leftTotal>rightTotal?"left":"right",safetyBlocker:{...safetyBlocker,any:safetyBlocker.left||safetyBlocker.right}};
+}

```

### tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-replay-boundary.ts

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-replay-boundary.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-replay-boundary.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..9af42c3b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-replay-boundary.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+import {prepareApprovedReplayFixture, type ReplayFixture} from "./context-ab-approved-replay.ts";
+
+/**
+ * Choose a replay boundary from a locally reviewed conversation.
+ * Replays the last user turn and only earlier context, never future turns.
+ * This is an offline utility: no database, auth, network or model calls.
+ */
+export function selectLastUserReplayBoundary(input: unknown): ReplayFixture {
+  if (!Array.isArray(input)) throw new Error("Expected conversation array");
+  let lastUser=-1;
+  for(let i=input.length-1;i>=0;i--) {
+    const item=input[i];
+    if(item && typeof item==="object" && !Array.isArray(item) && item.role==="user") {
+      lastUser=i;
+      break;
+    }
+  }
+  if(lastUser<2) throw new Error("Not enough context before last user turn");
+  return prepareApprovedReplayFixture(input.slice(0,lastUser+1));
+}

```

```text
diff --git a/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-replay-preflight.ts b/tests/three-block/vendor/slimming/lib/context-ab-replay-preflight.ts
new file mode 100644
```
